Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the machine_learning space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 658 and the convergence rate is O(105/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the machine_learning space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 658 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 105. QED.
